# Skills · X · part 8

851 entries, xpp-dataentityview to xuanzang. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **xpp-dataentityview**: Use when authoring or modifying a D365 F&O data entity (AxDataEntityView). Data entities are the OData/DMF writable layer over tables — they project columns from a backing query (or inlined data sources), expose natural keys for OData URL identity, and support write-through to underlying tables. Covers mapped vs computed columns, natural keys, the relationship between Query/ViewMetadata.DataSources, and DMF staging. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-dataentityview))
- **xpp-db-sync**: Synchronise the AxDB schema after X++ table, view or data entity changes on a Dynamics 365 F&O dev VM, as a partial sync of the changed tables (MCP build_d365fo_project dbSync, or d365fo.tools Invoke-D365DbSyncPartial / Invoke-D365DbSyncModule). Additive only; destructive changes need the user to name them. ([Verosoft-Design/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Verosoft-Design/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/dynamics-fno-xpp/skills/xpp-db-sync))
- **xpp-debug**: Use when reasoning from code and data alone has stalled and you need to SEE what the running system does — attach the X++ debugger to the AOS (w3wp) or the batch host, break in a method, read the call stack, locals and record buffers, step, evaluate expressions, then release. Covers the xpp_debug_* tools, how to trigger a hit, what to inspect, and the safety rules (a paused AOS freezes every session on the box). ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-debug))
- **xpp-edt**: Use when authoring or modifying a D365 F&O Extended Data Type (AxEdt). EDTs are the typedef layer — every well-authored table field references one. Covers all EDT primitive variants (string/int/int64/real/date/utcdatetime/timeofday/enum/guid/container/boolean), lookup EDTs with table references, array elements, inheritance via `Extends`, and the reuse-first rule. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-edt))
- **xpp-enum**: Use when authoring or modifying a D365 F&O base enum (AxEnum). Enums are the discrete-value typedefs of the platform — combo boxes on forms, filter values in queries, integer columns in SQL. Covers values, labels, persistence semantics, and the durable-API treatment of enum names. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-enum))
- **xpp-extension**: Use when authoring extensions to D365 F&O AOT objects — AxTableExtension, AxFormExtension, AxEdtExtension, AxEnumExtension, AxViewExtension, AxDataEntityViewExtension, AxMenuExtension — or X++ class extensions via [ExtensionOf] (Chain of Command). Extensions are the only legal way to modify Microsoft-shipped objects since release 8.0; this skill covers what each type can/can't do and the naming/structure conventions. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-extension))
- **xpp-feedback**: TRIGGER when you've hit acute friction with this plugin (tool naming confused you, schema was ambiguous, error message was cryptic, you reached for something that didn't exist, behavior surprised you) — or when the user explicitly asks you to reflect on your experience. Capture one focused observation as a feedback artifact in the plugin maintainer's inbox. The maintainer reads these to iterate the plugin; the value of your notes is proportional to how concrete and honest they are. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-feedback))

- **xpp-form-subpatterns**: Use whenever a pattern skill references a sub-pattern by name — Fields and Field Groups, Toolbar and List, Toolbar and Fields, Custom Filter Group, Nested Simple List and Details, the Workspace section sub-patterns, etc. Sub-patterns apply to container controls INSIDE a form (groups, FastTabs, tab pages) and dictate the legal contents and layout of that container. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-form-subpatterns))
- **xpp-labelfile**: Use when authoring or modifying labels in D365 F&O — label files (AxLabelFile), label resource text files, individual labels, multi-language label sets. Labels are the localization layer; every user-facing string in F&O should reference a label rather than embedding literal text. Use the xpp_label_* MCP tools for all reads and mutations — never Read or Edit .label.txt files directly. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-labelfile))
- **xpp-language**: TRIGGER when about to call any xpp_* MCP tool, or when the user mentions dynamics-xpp, dynamics-xpp, X++, AOT, or D365 F&O. Anchor skill for the plugin — without it, MCP tool calls miss the X++ language foundations, naming conventions, and dispatch to per-AOT-type and per-pattern sub-skills. (The dynamics-xpp:xpp-setup skill is once-per-machine; this skill is needed every session that uses the plugin.) ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-language))
- **xpp-menu**: Use when authoring or modifying a D365 F&O navigation menu (AxMenu). Menus group menu items, sub-menus, separators, and tiles into navigable trees — they back module entry pages, navigation pane sections, and workspace tile grids. Different from menu items (the leaf "open this form/report/class" objects) — menus are the containers. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-menu))
- **xpp-menuitem**: TRIGGER when authoring an AxMenuItemDisplay / AxMenuItemAction / AxMenuItemOutput. These are the entry points that make forms, runnable classes, and reports reachable from the UI. Required whenever you create a form (display), a runnable (action), or an SSRS report controller (output) — without a menu item, the object is unreachable. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-menuitem))
- **xpp-navigation**: Use when an AOT object is too big to read or rewrite whole — a large form (SalesTable is ~1.5MB), a wide table (100s of fields/relations), a class with hundreds of methods. Teaches path-addressable navigation: orient with an outline, locate a path with find, zoom one subtree, and edit it surgically — without ever pulling or resending the untouched rest. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-navigation))
- **xpp-pattern-details-master**: Use when authoring a DetailsMaster-pattern form in D365 F&O — the canonical pattern for entity master records (customer, vendor, item). In modern F&O this pattern ALSO subsumes the old ListPage when there's a 1:1 list-to-detail correspondence — the form carries both grid view and details view in one. Two variants — basic and "w/ Standard Tabs" (for &gt;15 FastTabs grouped into categories). ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-details-master))
- **xpp-pattern-details-transaction**: Use when authoring a DetailsTransaction-pattern form in D365 F&O — the canonical "header + lines" transactional document form (sales order, purchase order, journal). In modern F&O the form carries THREE views — Header view, Line view, Grid view — switched via the panel-tab structure. Header view is compulsory even if initially redundant. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-details-transaction))
- **xpp-pattern-list-page**: Use when authoring a ListPage-pattern form in D365 F&O — but FIRST check whether you should be merging list + details into a single Details Master/Transaction form. ListPage is now discouraged when there's a 1:1 correspondence between list and details; current guidance reserves it for forms with NO backing details page or with multiple backing details pages (e.g. project quotations + sales quotations on the same list). ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-list-page))
- **xpp-pattern-simple-list**: Use when authoring a SimpleList-pattern form in D365 F&O — a bare list (grid) with no action pane heavy lifting, used for picker dialogs, embedded subforms, and small lookup-edit screens. Pair with dynamics-xpp:xpp-form for the envelope basics. Ship a quick-filter row above the grid; field group reuse keeps the column set lean. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-simple-list))
- **xpp-pattern-simple-list-details**: Use when authoring a SimpleListDetails-pattern form in D365 F&O — master/detail layout with the master pane on the left and a details pane for the selected row on the right. Three variants — List Grid (default), Tabular Grid, Tree — chosen by the master pane's content shape. Covers FactBox use, header group layout, view-mode default, and the framework-provided New/Delete/Edit buttons. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-simple-list-details))
- **xpp-pattern-table-of-contents**: Use when authoring a TableOfContents-pattern form in D365 F&O — a tabbed parameter/setup form with vertical tabs on the left, each tab carrying a functional area's settings. Canonical for the *Parameters forms (Accounts Receivable parameters, GL parameters, Inventory parameters, etc.). ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-table-of-contents))
- **xpp-pattern-task**: Legacy pattern. Use ONLY for migrating existing AX 2012 forms — DO NOT use for new forms. The Task (a.k.a. "Task Single") pattern is legacy in F&O; new equivalent functionality should use Dialog, Drop Dialog, Simple Details, or Details Master instead. This skill documents the pattern for migration / inspection only. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-task))
- **xpp-pattern-task-parent-child**: Legacy pattern. Use ONLY for migrating existing AX 2012 forms — DO NOT use for new forms. The TaskParentChild (a.k.a. "Task Double") pattern is legacy in F&O; new functionality should use Details Transaction, Dialog, or a refactored workflow instead. This skill documents the pattern for migration / inspection only. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-task-parent-child))
- **xpp-pattern-wizard**: Use when authoring a Wizard-pattern form in D365 F&O — a multi-step guided form that walks the user through a sequential workflow. Reserve for genuinely sequential workflows; users find wizards heavyweight, so prefer Task when one screen would do. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-wizard))
- **xpp-pattern-workspace-operational**: Use when authoring a WorkspaceOperational-pattern form in D365 F&O — an activity-focused workspace landing page. As of 10.0.25 the pattern was reorganized to scroll VERTICALLY (no more panorama / horizontal scrolling); content sections are now stacked vertically and use restyled FastTabs. Two variants — standard Operational workspace and Operational workspace w/Tabs (10.0.25+) for organizing into multiple top-level sections. ([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-pattern-workspace-operational))
- **xpp-performance-rules**: The performance rules every piece of X++ must satisfy as it is written — complexity budget, no N+1 round-trips, set-based over row-by-row, index-covered where clauses, no work inside loops, and the conditions that silently downgrade a set-based statement. Apply to EVERY implementation task that touches data or loops, alongside $xpp-best-practice-rules. Invoke before writing a select, a loop, a batch job, a CoC wrapper on a hot path, or any code that runs per-record. ([Sangtt-nwv/Sangtt-WMS](https://github.com/Sangtt-nwv/Sangtt-WMS/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/xpp-performance-rules) · 3 copies)

([yobryon/dynamics-tools](https://github.com/yobryon/dynamics-tools/tree/HEAD/plugins/xpp/skills/xpp-workspace-charts))

- **xpplab**: Simulate ODE models and compute bifurcation diagrams with XPP Lab, which runs XPPAUT's integrators and its AUTO continuation.
